Absolute position (discretized into 5 bins, time-varying)\n        # Clip position to valid range\n        pos_clipped = np.clip(trial_pos, 0, TRACK_LENGTH)\n        pos_disc = discretize_position(pos_clipped, n_bins=5)\n\n        # 3. Speed (discretized, time-varying)\n        speed_disc = discretize_speed(np.abs(trial_speed))\n\n        # 4. Lick (binary, time-varying)\n        lick_disc = trial_lick.astype(int)\n\n        # 5. Reward zone location (per-trial): 0=A, 1=B, 2=C\n        rz_loc = {'A': 0, 'B': 1, 'C': 2}[rz_label]\n\n        # 6. Reward outcome (per-trial): 0=no, 1=yes\n        rew_out = int(reward_outcomes[i])\n\n        # Output: (6, n_timepoints) for time-varying, per-trial values broadcast\n        output_data = np.zeros((6, n_timepoints), dtype=np.int64)\n        output_data[0, :] = dist_disc\n        output_data[1, :] = pos_disc\n        output_data[2, :] = speed_disc\n        output_data[3, :] = lick_disc\n        output_data[4, :] = rz_loc\n        output_data[5, :] = rew_out\n\n        neural_trials.append(neural)\n        input_trials.append(input_data)\n        output_trials.append(output_data)\n\n    if len(neural_trials) < 2:\n        print(f\"  Skipping {subject_id} ses-{session_num}: only {len(neural_trials)} valid trials after filtering\")\n        return None\n\n    return {\n        'neural': neural_trials,\n        'input': input_trials,\n        'output': output_trials,\n        'n_curated': n_curated,\n        'n_interneurons': n_interneurons,\n        'n_kept': n_kept,\n        'n_trials': len(neural_trials),\n        'imaging_rate': imaging_rate,\n        'zone_labels': zone_labels,\n        'reward_outcomes': reward_outcomes,\n        'env_per_trial': env_per_trial,\n        'lick_error_trials': error_trials,\n    }\n\n\ndef convert_all(data_dir, sample_only=False, max_sessions_per_subject=None):\n    \"\"\"Convert all NWB files to the target format.\"\"\"\n\n    # Discover subjects\n    subjects_dirs = sorted([d for d in os.listdir(data_dir)\n                           if d.startswith('sub-') and os.path.isdir(os.path.join(data_dir, d))])\n\n    subjects = [d.replace('sub-', '') for d in subjects_dirs]\n    print(f\"Found {len(subjects)} subjects: {subjects}\")\n\n    all_neural = []\n    all_input = []\n    all_output = []\n    subject_idx_list = []\n    brain_region_idx_list = []\n\n    total_sessions = 0\n    total_trials = 0\n    total_cells_kept = 0\n    total_interneurons = 0\n\n    for subj_i, (subj_dir, subj_id) in enumerate(zip(subjects_dirs, subjects)):\n        subj_path = os.path.join(data_dir, subj_dir)\n        nwb_files = sorted(glob(os.path.join(subj_path, '*.nwb')))\n\n        if max_sessions_per_subject:\n            nwb_files = nwb_files[:max_sessions_per_subject]\n\n        print(f\"\\nProcessing {subj_id} ({len(nwb_files)} sessions)...\")\n\n        for nwb_file in nwb_files:\n            fname = os.path.basename(nwb_file)\n            session_num = fname.split('ses-')[1].split('_')[0]\n\n            print(f\"  Session {session_num}...\", end=\" \")\n\n            result = convert_session(nwb_file, subj_id, session_num)\n\n            if result is None:\n                continue\n\n            all_neural.append(result['neural'])\n            all_input.append(result['input'])\n            all_output.append(result['output'])\n            subject_idx_list.append(subj_i)\n            # All cells are from CA1\n            brain_region_idx_list.append(np.zeros(result['n_kept'], dtype=int))\n\n            total_sessions += 1\n            total_trials += result['n_trials']\n            total_cells_kept += result['n_kept']\n            total_interneurons += result['n_interneurons']\n\n            print(f\"OK: {result['n_trials']} trials, {result['n_kept']} cells \"\n                  f\"({result['n_curated']} curated, {result['n_interneurons']} interneurons removed, \"\n                  f\"{len(result['lick_error_trials'])} lick error trials)\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Total: {total_sessions} sessions, {total_trials} trials, \"\n          f\"mean {total_cells_kept/total_sessions:.0f} cells/session\")\n    print(f\"Total interneurons removed: {total_interneurons}\")\n\n    # ---- Assemble final data dictionary ----\n    data = {\n        'neural': all_neural,\n        'input': all_input,\n        'output': all_output,\n\n        'subjects': subjects,\n        'subject_idx': np.array(subject_idx_list, dtype=int),\n\n        'brain_regions': ['CA1'],\n        'brain_region_idx': brain_region_idx_list,\n\n        'input_names': [\n            'time_from_trial_start',\n            'environment_type',\n            'trial_number',\n            'previous_trial_outcome',\n        ],\n\n        'output_names': [\n            'distance_to_reward_zone',\n            'absolute_position',\n            'speed',\n            'lick',\n            'reward_zone_location',\n            'reward_outcome',\n        ],\n\n        'output_values': [\n            ['< -50cm', '-50 to -10cm', '-10 to 0cm', '0cm (in zone)', '0 to +10cm', '+10 to +50cm', '> +50cm'],\n            ['0-90cm', '90-180cm', '180-270cm', '270-360cm', '360-450cm'],\n            ['< 2cm/s', '2-10cm/s', '10-20cm/s', '20-40cm/s', '> 40cm/s'],\n            ['no_lick', 'lick'],\n            ['zone_A', 'zone_B', 'zone_C'],\n            ['no_reward', 'reward'],\n        ],\n\n        'metadata': {\n            'task_description': 'Virtual reality navigation with hidden reward zones in hippocampal CA1 imaging. '\n                               'Mice navigate a 450cm linear track with one of three possible hidden reward zones '\n                               '(A: 80-130cm, B: 200-250cm, C: 320-370cm). Reward zone location switches across days.',\n            'time_bin_size': 1000.0 / 15.5078125,  # ms (1/imaging_rate * 1000)\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'start of trial (re-entry into virtual environment at position 0)',\n            'off_start': 0.0,\n            'off_end': None,  # Variable trial length\n            'track_length_cm': 450,\n            'reward_zones': REWARD_ZONES,\n            'n_subjects': len(subjects),\n            'n_sessions': total_sessions,\n            'n_trials_total': total_trials,\n            'imaging_rate_hz': 15.5078125,\n            'neural_data_type': 'deconvolved calcium activity (OASIS algorithm)',\n            'cell_filtering': 'Manual curation (Suite2P) + putative interneuron exclusion (dF/F-speed correlation > 0.5)',\n            'paper': 'Sosa, Plitt & Giocomo (2025) Nature Neuroscience',\n        }\n    }\n\n    return data\n\n\ndef create_sample(data, max_sessions=5, max_trials=20):\n    \"\"\"Create a smaller sample dataset for quick testing.\"\"\"\n    import copy\n    sample = copy.deepcopy(data)\n\n    # Limit sessions\n    n_sessions = min(max_sessions, len(data['neural']))\n\n    sample['neural'] = []\n    sample['input'] = []\n    sample['output'] = []\n    sample['brain_region_idx'] = []\n    subject_idx = []\n\n    for i in range(n_sessions):\n        n_trials = min(max_trials, len(data['neural'][i]))\n        sample['neural'].append(data['neural'][i][:n_trials])\n        sample['input'].append(data['input'][i][:n_trials])\n        sample['output'].append(data['output'][i][:n_trials])\n        sample['brain_region_idx'].append(data['brain_region_idx'][i])\n        subject_idx.append(data['subject_idx'][i])\n\n    sample['subject_idx'] = np.array(subject_idx, dtype=int)\n\n    return sample\n\n\ndef print_sanity_checks(data):\n    \"\"\"Print sanity checks comparing our data to the paper's statistics.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"SANITY CHECKS\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    n_sessions = len(data['neural'])\n    n_subjects = len(data['subjects'])\n\n    # Paper says: 11 switch mice, 14 days each (but m11 starts day 3 = 12 sessions)\n    print(f\"\\n1. Number of subjects: {n_subjects} (paper: 11 switch mice)\")\n\n    # Sessions per subject\n    for subj in range(n_subjects):\n        n_sess = np.sum(data['subject_idx'] == subj)\n        print(f\"   {data['subjects'][subj]}: {n_sess} sessions\")\n\n    # Paper says: mean 80.5 +/- 7.4 trials per session\n    trials_per_session = [len(data['neural'][i]) for i in range(n_sessions)]\n    print(f\"\\n2. Trials per session: mean={np.mean(trials_per_session):.1f} \"\n          f\"+/- {np.std(trials_per_session):.1f} (paper: 80.5 +/- 7.4)\")\n\n    # Paper says: 155-2172 neurons per session\n    neurons_per_session = [data['neural'][i][0].shape[0] for i in range(n_sessions)]\n    print(f\"\\n3. Neurons per session: range=[{min(neurons_per_session)}, {max(neurons_per_session)}] \"\n          f\"(paper: 155-2172)\")\n    print(f\"   Mean: {np.mean(neurons_per_session):.0f}\")\n\n    # Imaging rate\n    print(f\"\\n4. Time bin size: {data['metadata']['time_bin_size']:.2f} ms \"\n          f\"(= 1/{data['metadata']['imaging_rate_hz']:.1f} Hz)\")\n\n    # Reward omission rate (paper: ~15%)\n    all_outcomes = []\n    for i in range(n_sessions):\n        for t in range(len(data['output'][i])):\n            all_outcomes.append(data['output'][i][t][5, 0])  # reward_outcome\n    all_outcomes = np.array(all_outcomes)\n    omission_rate = 1 - np.mean(all_outcomes)\n    print(f\"\\n5. Reward omission rate: {omission_rate*100:.1f}% (paper: ~15%)\")\n\n    # Check reward zone distribution\n    rz_counts = {0: 0, 1: 0, 2: 0}\n    for i in range(n_sessions):\n        for t in range(len(data['output'][i])):\n            rz = int(data['output'][i][t][4, 0])\n            rz_counts[rz] += 1\n    total = sum(rz_counts.values())\n    print(f\"\\n6. Reward zone distribution: A={rz_counts[0]/total*100:.1f}%, \"\n          f\"B={rz_counts[1]/total*100:.1f}%, C={rz_counts[2]/total*100:.1f}%\")\n\n    # Environment distribution\n    env_counts = {0: 0, 1: 0}\n    for i in range(n_sessions):\n        for t in range(len(data['input'][i])):\n            env_val = int(data['input'][i][t][1, 0])\n            env_counts[env_val] = env_counts.get(env_val, 0) + 1\n    total = sum(env_counts.values())\n    print(f\"\\n7. Environment distribution: ENV1={env_counts.get(0,0)/total*100:.1f}%, \"\n          f\"ENV2={env_counts.get(1,0)/total*100:.1f}%\")\n\n    # Speed distribution check\n    print(f\"\\n8. Speed bin distribution (sample):\")\n    speed_counts = np.zeros(5)\n    for i in range(min(5, n_sessions)):\n        for t in range(len(data['output'][i])):\n            for b in range(5):\n                speed_counts[b] += np.sum(data['output'][i][t][2, :] == b)\n    total = speed_counts.sum()\n    speed_labels = ['<2', '2-10', '10-20', '20-40', '>40']\n    for b in range(5):\n        print(f\"   {speed_labels[b]} cm/s: {speed_counts[b]/total*100:.1f}%\")\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# Main\n# ============================================================\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    import argparse\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Convert NWB data to decoder format')\n    parser.add_argument('--sample-only', action='store_true',\n                       help='Only create sample dataset (faster)')\n    parser.add_argument('--max-sessions', type=int, default=None,\n                       help='Max sessions per subject')\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"Converting NWB data to decoder format...\")\n    print(f\"Data directory: {DATA_DIR}\")\n\n    data = convert_all(DATA_DIR, sample_only=args.sample_only,\n                       max_sessions_per_subject=args.max_sessions)\n\n    # The 3 \"fixed-condition\" mice (GCAMP2, GCAMP6, GCAMP10) are not included in the DANDI dataset.\n\n| NWB Subject | Code Name | Sessions | Starting Env | Notes |\n|-------------|-----------|----------|-------------|-------|\n| sub-m3  | GCAMP3  | 14 (days 1-14)  | ENV1 | |\n| sub-m4  | GCAMP4  | 14 (days 1-14)  | ENV1 | |\n| sub-m7  | GCAMP7  | 14 (days 1-14)  | ENV1 | |\n| sub-m11 | GCAMP11 | 12 (days 3-14)  | ENV1 | No imaging days 1-2 |\n| sub-m12 | GCAMP12 | 14 (days 1-14)  | ENV1 | |\n| sub-m13 | GCAMP13 | 14 (days 1-14)  | ENV1 | |\n| sub-m14 | GCAMP14 | 14 (days 1-14)  | ENV1 | |\n| sub-m15 | GCAMP15 | 14 (days 1-14)  | ENV1 | |\n| sub-m17 | GCAMP17 | 14 (days 1-14)  | ENV2 | Multi-plane imaging |\n| sub-m18 | GCAMP18 | 14 (days 1-14)  | ENV2 | Multi-plane imaging |\n| sub-m19 | GCAMP19 | 14 (days 1-14)  | ENV1 | |\n\n## Neural Data Processing\n\n### Source\n- **Deconvolved calcium activity** from NWB (`processing/ophys/Deconvolved/plane0/data`)\n- Already processed via OASIS algorithm applied to dF/F (as described in the paper)\n- For multi-plane sessions (m17, m18): plane0 and plane1 data concatenated\n\n### Cell Filtering\n1. **Manual curation**: Only cells with `iscell[:,0] == 1` included (Suite2P manual curation)\n2. **Interneuron exclusion**: Cells with Pearson correlation > 0.5 between simplified dF/F and running speed are excluded\n   - dF/F computed from raw fluorescence with neuropil subtraction (coef=0.7) and maximin baseline\n   - Paper reports 0.42 +/- 0.85% excluded; our conversion removed 119 total interneurons across all sessions\n\n### Cell Counts\n- Range: 155-2339 neurons per session (paper reports 155-2172)\n- Mean: 912 neurons per session\n- Slight excess over paper range due to multi-plane pooling in m17/m18\n\n## Trial Structure\n\n### Trial Boundaries\n- Start: `trial_start` signal (re-entry into virtual environment at position 0)\n- End: `teleport` signal (end of trial, entering teleport zone)\n- Temporal alignment: start of each trial\n\n### Trial Counts\n- Mean: 80.4 +/- 6.1 trials per session (paper: 80.5 +/- 7.4)\n- Total: 12,216 trials across 152 sessions\n- Target was 80-100 trials per session\n\n## Behavioral Data Processing\n\n### Lick Sensor Error Correction\n- Following the reference code (`behavior.py:correct_lick_sensor_error`):\n- Trials where >35% of samples have cumulative lick count >2 are flagged as sensor errors\n- Lick data on these trials set to 0\n- Paper reports ~0.65% of all imaged trials affected (81/12,376)\n\n### Speed\n- Running speed from NWB (`speed` field), already aligned to imaging frames\n- Absolute value used for discretization\n\n### Reward Zone Detection\n- Reward zone location determined from position data when `reward_zone` field > 0\n- Zone A: 80-130 cm, Zone B: 200-250 cm, Zone C: 320-370 cm\n- For omission trials (no rzone activation), zone inherited from neighboring trials via forward/backward fill\n\n### Environment Type\n- Directly from NWB `environment` field: 0 = ENV1, 1 = ENV2\n- Verified for m17/m18 which start in ENV2\n\n### Reward Outcome\n- Determined by matching sparse reward event timestamps to trial time windows\n- Omission rate: 15.3% (paper: ~15%)\n\n## Decoder Variables\n\n### Inputs (4 dimensions, all time-varying)\n1. **time_from_trial_start**: Time in seconds from trial start (0 at trial onset)\n2. **environment_type**: Binary (0=ENV1, 1=ENV2), constant within trial\n3. **trial_number**: 1-indexed trial number within session, constant within trial\n4. **previous_trial_outcome**: Binary (0=omission, 1=rewarded), constant within trial; first trial of session = 0\n\n### Outputs (6 dimensions)\n1. **distance_to_reward_zone** (7 classes, time-varying):\n   - Signed distance to nearest edge of reward zone\n   - 0: < -50cm, 1: -50 to -10cm, 2: -10 to <0cm, 3: 0cm (in zone), 4: >0 to +10cm, 5: +10 to +50cm, 6: >+50cm\n2. **absolute_position** (5 classes, time-varying):\n   - Position discretized into 5 equal 90cm bins (0-90, 90-180, 180-270, 270-360, 360-450)\n3. **speed** (5 classes, time-varying):\n   - 0: <2cm/s, 1: 2-10cm/s, 2: 10-20cm/s, 3: 20-40cm/s, 4: >40cm/s\n4. **lick** (2 classes, time-varying):\n   - Binary: 0=no lick, 1=lick detected in this frame\n5. **reward_zone_location** (3 classes, per-trial):\n   - 0=Zone A, 1=Zone B, 2=Zone C\n6. **reward_outcome** (2 classes, per-trial):\n   - 0=no reward (omission), 1=reward delivered\n\n## Sanity Checks\n\n| Metric | Our Data | Paper |\n|--------|----------|-------|\n| Number of subjects | 11 | 11 (switch mice) |\n| Sessions per subject | 12-14 | 14 (m11 starts day 3) |\n| Trials per session | 80.4 +/- 6.1 | 80.5 +/- 7.4 |\n| Neurons per session | 155-2339 | 155-2172 |\n| Imaging rate | 15.5 Hz | ~15.5 Hz |\n| Reward omission rate | 15.3% | ~15% |\n| Reward zone distribution | A:34.3%, B:32.8%, C:32.9% | ~equal |\n| Environment distribution | ENV1:51.0%, ENV2:49.0% | ~equal |\n| Interneurons excluded | 119 total | 0.42 +/- 0.85% |\n\n## Decoder Performance (Full Data, 152 sessions)\n\n| Output | Train Balanced Acc | Val Balanced Acc | Chance |\n|--------|-------------------|-----------------|--------|\n| distance_to_reward_zone | 0.377 | 0.345 | 0.143 |\n| 0.993 | 0.333 |\n| reward_outcome | 0.668 | 0.428 | 0.500 |\n\n## Time Bin Size\n- 64.48 ms (1/15.5078125 Hz)\n- Matches the 2-photon imaging frame rate\n\n## Key Decisions\n1. Used deconvolved activity (not dF/F) as neural data, matching the paper's use of deconvolved activity for most analyses\n2. Multi-plane data (m17, m18) concatenated across planes, following the paper's approach of pooling planes for all analyses except Extended Data Fig. 7\n3. Trial data includes only the on-track period (trial_start to teleport), excluding teleport zone\n4. No speed threshold applied to neural data for the decoder (the paper applies speed < 2 cm/s threshold for spatial analyses, but the decoder benefits from all timepoints)\n5. Lick data binarized (0/1) after sensor error correction\n=====README=====\n# Neural Decoder Data Conversion\n\nConversion of hippocampal CA1 2-photon calcium imaging data from Sosa, Plitt & Giocomo (2025) \"A flexible hippocampal population code for experience relative to reward\" (Nature Neuroscience) into a standardized format for neural decoder training.\n\n## Data Source\n- **DANDI Archive**: Dandiset 001361\n- **Format**: Neurodata Without Borders (NWB)\n- **Reference code**: https://github.com/GiocomoLab/Sosa_et_al_2024\n\n## Files\n\n| File | Description |\n|------|-------------|\n| `convert_data.py` | Main conversion script |\n| `converted_data.pkl` | Full converted dataset (152 sessions, ~9 GB) |\n| `sample_data.pkl` | Sample dataset for quick testing (5 sessions, ~14 MB) |\n| `CONVERSION_NOTES.md` | Detailed conversion decisions and validation |\n| `train_decoder.py` | Decoder training and validation script |\n| `decoder.py` | Decoder model implementation |\n| `conversion_full_out.txt` | Full conversion log |\n| `conversion_sample_out.txt` | Sample data verification log |\n| `verification_full_out.txt` | Full data format verification log |\n| `verification_sample_out.txt` | Sample data format verification log |\n| `train_decoder_full_out.txt` | Full decoder training output |\n| `train_decoder_sample_out.txt` | Sample decoder training output |\n\n## Task Description\n\nMice navigate a 450cm virtual linear track with a hidden reward zone at one of three possible locations (A: 80-130cm, B: 200-250cm, C: 320-370cm). The reward zone switches locations across sessions. Two distinct virtual environments (ENV1, ENV2) are used.\n\n## Data Structure\n\n```python\ndata = {\n    'neural': [[trial_array, ...], ...],  # (n_neurons, T) per trial\n    'input': [[input_array, ...], ...],    # (4, T) per trial\n    'output': [[output_array, ...], ...],  # (6, T) per trial\n    'subjects': ['m11', 'm12', ...],       # 11 subjects\n    'subject_idx': np.array([...]),        # session-to-subject mapping\n    'brain_regions': ['CA1'],\n    'brain_region_idx': [...],\n    'input_names': ['time_from_trial_start', 'environment_type', 'trial_number', 'previous_trial_outcome'],\n    'output_names': ['distance_to_reward_zone', 'absolute_position', 'speed', 'lick', 'reward_zone_location', 'reward_outcome'],\n    'output_values': [[...], ...],         # class labels for each output\n    'metadata': {...}\n}\n```\n\n## Usage\n\n```bash\n# Convert NWB data to decoder format\npython convert_data.py\n\n# Verify data format\npython train_decoder.py converted_data.pkl --verify-only\n\n# Train decoder\npython train_decoder.py converted_data.pkl --cpu\n\n# Quick test with sample data\npython train_decoder.py sample_data.pkl --cpu --plot-samples\n```\n\n## Dataset Statistics\n\n- **11 subjects**, 152 sessions, 12,216 trials\n- **912 neurons/session** on average (range: 155-2339)\n- **80.4 trials/session** on average\n- **15.5 Hz** imaging rate (64.48 ms time bins)\n- **15.3%** reward omission rate","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a2c84688-f2eb-446c-ba7a-6fd27d122b80","uuid":"fce0806f-71f4-44d6-b20f-c0d82ae4ae49","timestamp":"2026-09-23T22:12:51.953Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"# Data Conversion Notes\n\n## Source Data\n- **Paper**: Sosa, Plitt & Giocomo (2025) \"A flexible hippocampal population code for experience relative to reward\", Nature Neuroscience\n- **Data**: DANDI Archive, Dandiset 001361, NWB format\n- **Code**: Reference analysis code from https://github.com/GiocomoLab/Sosa_et_al_2024\n\n## Subject Mapping\nThe NWB data contains 11 \"switch task\" mice (sub-m3, m4, m7, m11-m15, m17-m19), corresponding to GCAMP3, GCAMP4, GCAMP7, GCAMP11-GCAMP15, GCAMP17-GCAMP19 in the reference code. our conversion removed 119 total interneurons across all sessions\n\n### Cell Counts\n- Range: 155-2339 neurons per session (paper reports 155-2172)\n- Mean: 912 neurons per session\n- Slight excess over paper range due to multi-plane pooling in m17/m18\n\n## Trial Structure\n\n### Trial Boundaries\n- Start: `trial_start` signal (re-entry into virtual environment at position 0)\n- End: `teleport` signal (end of trial, entering teleport zone)\n- Temporal alignment: start of each trial\n\n### Trial Counts\n- Mean: 80.4 +/- 6.1 trials per session (paper: 80.5 +/- 7.4)\n- Total: 12,216 trials across 152 sessions\n- Target was 80-100 trials per session\n\n## Behavioral Data Processing\n\n### Lick Sensor Error Correction\n- Following the reference code (`behavior.py:correct_lick_sensor_error`):\n- Trials where >35% of samples have cumulative lick count >2 are flagged as sensor errors\n- Lick data on these trials set to 0\n- Paper reports ~0.65% of all imaged trials affected (81/12,376)\n\n### Speed\n- Running speed from NWB (`speed` field), already aligned to imaging frames\n- Absolute value used for discretization\n\n### Reward Zone Detection\n- Reward zone location determined from position data when `reward_zone` field > 0\n- Zone A: 80-130 cm, Zone B: 200-250 cm, Zone C: 320-370 cm\n- For omission trials (no rzone activation), zone inherited from neighboring trials via forward/backward fill\n\n### Environment Type\n- Directly from NWB `environment` field: 0 = ENV1, 1 = ENV2\n- Verified for m17/m18 which start in ENV2\n\n### Reward Outcome\n- Determined by matching sparse reward event timestamps to trial time windows\n- Omission rate: 15.3% (paper: ~15%)\n\n## Decoder Variables\n\n### Inputs (4 dimensions, all time-varying)\n1. **time_from_trial_start**: Time in seconds from trial start (0 at trial onset)\n2. **environment_type**: Binary (0=ENV1, 1=ENV2), constant within trial\n3. **trial_number**: 1-indexed trial number within session, constant within trial\n4. **previous_trial_outcome**: Binary (0=omission, 1=rewarded), constant within trial; first trial of session = 0\n\n### Outputs (6 dimensions)\n1. **distance_to_reward_zone** (7 classes, time-varying):\n   - Signed distance to nearest edge of reward zone\n   - 0: < -50cm, 1: -50 to -10cm, 2: -10 to <0cm, 3: 0cm (in zone), 4: >0 to +10cm, 5: +10 to +50cm, 6: >+50cm\n2. **absolute_position** (5 classes, time-varying):\n   - Position discretized into 5 equal 90cm bins (0-90, 90-180, 180-270, 270-360, 360-450)\n3. **speed** (5 classes, time-varying):\n   - 0: <2cm/s, 1: 2-10cm/s, 2: 10-20cm/s, 3: 20-40cm/s, 4: >40cm/s\n4. **lick** (2 classes, time-varying):\n   - Binary: 0=no lick, 1=lick detected in this frame\n5. **reward_zone_location** (3 classes, per-trial):\n   - 0=Zone A, 1=Zone B, 2=Zone C\n6. **reward_outcome** (2 classes, per-trial):\n   - 0=no reward (omission), 1=reward delivered\n\n## Sanity Checks\n\n| Metric | Our Data | Paper |\n|--------|----------|-------|\n| Number of subjects | 11 | 11 (switch mice) |\n| Sessions per subject | 12-14 | 14 (m11 starts day 3) |\n| Trials per session | 80.4 +/- 6.1 | 80.5 +/- 7.4 |\n| Neurons per session | 155-2339 | 155-2172 |\n| Imaging rate | 15.5 Hz | ~15.5 Hz |\n| Reward omission rate | 15.3% | ~15% |\n| Reward zone distribution | A:34.3%, B:32.8%, C:32.9% | ~equal |\n| Environment distribution | ENV1:51.0%, ENV2:49.0% | ~equal |\n| Interneurons excluded | 119 total | 0.42 +/- 0.85% |\n\n## Decoder Performance (Full Data, 152 sessions)\n\n| Output | Train Balanced Acc | Val Balanced Acc | Chance |\n|--------|-------------------|-----------------|--------|\n| distance_to_reward_zone | 0.377 | 0.345 | 0.143 |\n| absolute_position | 0.541 | 0.517 | 0.200 |\n| speed | 0.444 | 0.415 | 0.200 |\n| lick | 0.651 | 0.641 | 0.500 |\n| reward_zone_location | 0.842 | 0.800 | 0.333 |\n| reward_outcome | 0.556 | 0.525 | 0.500 |\n\n## Decoder Performance (Sample Data, 5 sessions)\n\n| Output | Train Balanced Acc | Val Balanced Acc | Chance |\n|--------|-------------------|-----------------|--------|\n| distance_to_reward_zone | 0.507 | 0.387 | 0.143 |\n| absolute_position | 0.561 | 0.523 | 0.200 |\n| speed | 0.425 | 0.315 | 0.200 |\n| lick | 0.713 | 0.636 | 0.500 |\n| reward_zone_location | 0.995 | 0.993 | 0.333 |\n| reward_outcome | 0.668 | 0.428 | 0.500 |\n\n## Time Bin Size\n- 64.48 ms (1/15.5078125 Hz)\n- Matches the 2-photon imaging frame rate\n\n## Key Decisions\n1. Used deconvolved activity (not dF/F) as neural data, matching the paper's use of deconvolved activity for most analyses\n2. Multi-plane data (m17, m18) concatenated across planes, following the paper's approach of pooling planes for all analyses except Extended Data Fig. 7\n3. Trial data includes only the on-track period (trial_start to teleport), excluding teleport zone\n4. No speed threshold applied to neural data for the decoder (the paper applies speed < 2 cm/s threshold for spatial analyses, but the decoder benefits from all timepoints)\n5. Lick data binarized (0/1) after sensor error correction\n=====README=====\n# Neural Decoder Data Conversion\n\nConversion of hippocampal CA1 2-photon calcium imaging data from Sosa, Plitt & Giocomo (2025) \"A flexible hippocampal population code for experience relative to reward\" (Nature Neuroscience) into a standardized format for neural decoder training.\n\n## Data Source\n- **DANDI Archive**: Dandiset 001361\n- **Format**: Neurodata Without Borders (NWB)\n- **Reference code**: https://github.com/GiocomoLab/Sosa_et_al_2024\n\n## Files\n\n| File | Description |\n|------|-------------|\n| `convert_data.py` | Main conversion script |\n| `converted_data.pkl` | Full converted dataset (152 sessions, ~9 GB) |\n| `sample_data.pkl` | Sample dataset for quick testing (5 sessions, ~14 MB) |\n| `CONVERSION_NOTES.md` | Detailed conversion decisions and validation |\n| `train_decoder.py` | Decoder training and validation script |\n| `decoder.py` | Decoder model implementation |\n| `conversion_full_out.txt` | Full conversion log |\n| `conversion_sample_out.txt` | Sample data verification log |\n| `verification_full_out.txt` | Full data format verification log |\n| `verification_sample_out.txt` | Sample data format verification log |\n| `train_decoder_full_out.txt` | Full decoder training output |\n| `train_decoder_sample_out.txt` | Sample decoder training output |\n\n## Task Description\n\nMice navigate a 450cm virtual linear track with a hidden reward zone at one of three possible locations (A: 80-130cm, B: 200-250cm, C: 320-370cm). The reward zone switches locations across sessions. Two distinct virtual environments (ENV1, ENV2) are used.\n\n## Data Structure\n\n```python\ndata = {\n    'neural': [[trial_array, ...], ...],  # (n_neurons, T) per trial\n    'input': [[input_array, ...], ...],    # (4, T) per trial\n    'output': [[output_array, ...], ...],  # (6, T) per trial\n    'subjects': ['m11', 'm12', ...],       # 11 subjects\n    'subject_idx': np.array([...]),        # session-to-subject mapping\n    'brain_regions': ['CA1'],\n    'brain_region_idx': [...],\n    'input_names': ['time_from_trial_start', 'environment_type', 'trial_number', 'previous_trial_outcome'],\n    'output_names': ['distance_to_reward_zone', 'absolute_position', 'speed', 'lick', 'reward_zone_location', 'reward_outcome'],\n    'output_values': [[...], ...],         # class labels for each output\n    'metadata': {...}\n}\n```\n\n## Usage\n\n```bash\n# Convert NWB data to decoder format\npython convert_data.py\n\n# Verify data format\npython train_decoder.py converted_data.pkl --verify-only\n\n# Train decoder\npython train_decoder.py converted_data.pkl --cpu\n\n# Quick test with sample data\npython train_decoder.py sample_data.pkl --cpu --plot-samples\n```\n\n## Dataset Statistics\n\n- **11 subjects**, 152 sessions, 12,216 trials\n- **912 neurons/session** on average (range: 155-2339)\n- **80.4 trials/session** on average\n- **15.5 Hz** imaging rate (64.48 ms time bins)\n- **15.3%** reward omission rate","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

ms\n\n    behavior_data = nwbfile.processing['behavior']['BehavioralTimeSeries'].time_series\n    \n    timestamps = behavior_data['trial number'].timestamps[:] \n    if T != len(timestamps):\n        print(f\"Warning: number of neural time bins ({T}) does not match number of behavior timestamps ({len(timestamps)}), cropping to the minimum of the two\")\n        T = min(T, len(timestamps))\n        fluorescence = [f[:T] for f in fluorescence]\n        neuropil = [f[:T] for f in neuropil]\n        timestamps = timestamps[:T]\n    \n    behavior_time_bin_size = np.median(np.diff(timestamps))*1000 # ms\n    \n    # trial number for each time point\n    trial_start_idx, trial_end_idx = get_trial_starts_ends(behavior_data['trial_start'].data[:], behavior_data['teleport'].data[:])\n    ntrials = len(trial_start_idx)\n    \n    # reward_amounts are all the same\n    reward_amounts = behavior_data['Reward'].data[:][:T]\n    assert np.allclose(reward_amounts,reward_amounts[0]), f\"Reward amounts are not all the same, unique values: {np.unique(reward_amounts)}\"\n    reward_times = behavior_data['Reward'].timestamps[:] # autoreward is all 0s, so not useful for modeling, but check that it is all zeros\n\n    # vr environment\n    vr_environment = behavior_data['environment'].data[:][:T] # one value per time point, does this change by session?\n    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['environment'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for environment do not match trial number timestamps\"\n\n    # current position in the VR environment\n    position = behavior_data['position'].data[:][:T]\n    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['position'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for position do not match trial number timestamps\"\n\n    # exactly the two groups this table holds -- \"except for mice m11-m14 on task days 1, 7, 8 and\n    # 14, and mice m15-m19 on day 1 and all switch days\" -- and names m17 and m18 as the two\n    # animals imaged in two planes, which are the two-plane sessions in this dataset. Unshifted, the last sample of every\n        # lap comes out NaN (80 per session here) and one pre-lap sample gets a value instead;\n        # shifted, dff's windows land exactly on the emitted laps. The one cost is that on\n        # keep_teleports sessions the baseline window opens two samples after the teleport onset\n        # rather than their one.\n        dff_curr, events_curr = dff(fluorescence_curr.T.astype(np.float64),\n                                    pd.Series(trial_start_idx + 1), pd.Series(trial_end_idx + 1),\n                                    f_neu=neuropil_curr.T.astype(np.float64),\n                                    neuropil_method='subtract', baseline_method='maximin',\n                                    subtract_baseline=True, neu_coef=0.7, tau=0.7,\n                                    frame_rate=frame_rate, n_planes=nplanes, deconvolve=True,\n                                    keep_teleports=keep_teleports)\n        # neural data\n        neural.append(spike_data_curr.T)\n\n        # time from trial start\n        input_curr = np.full((ninputs, ntimepoints), np.nan)\n        input_curr[0] = timestamps_curr - timestamps_curr[0]\n        \n        #
